[](undefined) 4750
[](undefined) 4751/*
[](undefined) 4752 * DISCLAIMER: the mid-size code path built on XXH3_mix16B() (lengths 17 to 240)
[](undefined) 4753 * has known collision weaknesses. XXH3 is a non-cryptographic hash: collision
[](undefined) 4754 * resistance is not one of its guarantees, and the seed (or custom secret) only
[](undefined) 4755 * makes intentional collisions *harder*, it is not a security boundary.
[](undefined) 4756 * These weaknesses are nonetheless documented here, so that users relying on a
[](undefined) 4757 * seed for a low per-pair collision probability know what to expect.
[](undefined) 4758 *
[](undefined) 4759 * 1) XXH3_mul128_fold64() does not separate complemented multiplicands.
[](undefined) 4760 *    Modulo 2^128, and with s = a + b + 1 :
[](undefined) 4761 *        (~a) * (~b) == a*b + s - (s + 1) * 2^64
[](undefined) 4762 *    so the low half of the product gains `s` while the high half loses `s`.
[](undefined) 4763 *    Since carry chains are short, both halves happen to flip the same bits
[](undefined) 4764 *    fairly often, leaving their xor (the "fold") unchanged with probability
[](undefined) 4765 *    ~2^-27, instead of the ideal 2^-64. Complementing a single multiplicand
[](undefined) 4766 *    behaves the same way.
[](undefined) 4767 *
[](undefined) 4768 *    Consequence for XXH3_64bits(): for any length in [32, 240], two messages
[](undefined) 4769 *    differing only by the complement of their first 8 (or first 16) bytes
[](undefined) 4770 *    collide with probability ~2^-27 over a uniformly random secret. The
[](undefined) 4771 *    _withSeed variant is also affected, but its collision probability depends
[](undefined) 4772 *    on the chosen pair because the 64-bit seed produces a structured secret.
[](undefined) 4773 *    A fixed pair has been measured to collide with probability ~2^-21 over
[](undefined) 4774 *    random seeds. This is an observed result, not a known upper bound.
[](undefined) 4775 *
[](undefined) 4776 *    Consequence for XXH128(): XXH128_mix32B() mixes each 16-byte chunk a
[](undefined) 4777 *    second time as a raw 64-bit sum, which normally breaks the pattern above.
[](undefined) 4778 *    But that sum is *also* invariant under complementation when
[](undefined) 4779 *    w0 + w1 == 2^64 - 1, and under that additional constraint both output
[](undefined) 4780 *    halves collide simultaneously, again with probability ~2^-27 over a
[](undefined) 4781 *    uniformly random secret. The seeded variant is also affected, and its
[](undefined) 4782 *    exact probability can similarly depend on the chosen pair.
[](undefined) 4783 *    So the 128-bit variant is _not_ immune, contrarily to what was previously
[](undefined) 4784 *    believed, and its effective margin here (2^-27 vs. an ideal 2^-128) is
[](undefined) 4785 *    even thinner than the 64-bit one.
[](undefined) 4786 *
[](undefined) 4787 *    Detailed analysis and measurements in
[](undefined) 4788 *    https://github.com/Cyan4973/xxHash/issues/1127 .
[](undefined) 4789 *
[](undefined) 4790 * 2) *seed-dependent* multicollisions, due to multiplication by zero: when one
[](undefined) 4791 *    of the two multiplicands below is zero, the whole 16-byte chunk stops
[](undefined) 4792 *    contributing to the hash, so all chunks sharing this property collide.
[](undefined) 4793 *    For random inputs, this is a 1 in 2^63 chance of 8 consecutive bytes
[](undefined) 4794 *    cancelling out, in a function invoked at most 16 times per hash, hence
[](undefined) 4795 *    essentially unreachable by accident. But it is trivially reachable by an
[](undefined) 4796 *    attacker who knows the secret (i.e. the unseeded variants). For
[](undefined) 4797 *    comparison, classic UMAC takes a 1 in 2^31 chance of 4 consecutive bytes
[](undefined) 4798 *    cancelling out the secret an arbitrary number of times (addressed for
[](undefined) 4799 *    long inputs in XXH3_accumulate_512).
[](undefined) 4800 *
[](undefined) 4801 * None of the above can be fixed without changing XXH3_mix16B(), which would
[](undefined) 4802 * change every hash value in the [17, 240] range, and XXH3 output values are
[](undefined) 4803 * now frozen. Therefore, if adversarial collision resistance matters for your
[](undefined) 4804 * use case, use a cryptographic hash or a MAC instead.
[](undefined) 4805 */
[](undefined) 4806XXH_FORCE_INLINE xxh_u64 XXH3_mix16B(const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 4807                                     const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret, xxh_u64 seed64)
[](undefined) 4808{
[](undefined) 4809#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__) /* GCC, not Clang */ \
[](undefined) 4810  && defined(__i386__) && defined(__SSE2__)  /* x86 + SSE2 */ \
[](undefined) 4811  && !defined(XXH_ENABLE_AUTOVECTORIZE)      /* Define to disable like XXH32 hack */
[](undefined) 4812    /*
[](undefined) 4813     * UGLY HACK:
[](undefined) 4814     * GCC for x86 tends to autovectorize the 128-bit multiply, resulting in
[](undefined) 4815     * slower code.
[](undefined) 4816     *
[](undefined) 4817     * By forcing seed64 into a register, we disrupt the cost model and
[](undefined) 4818     * cause it to scalarize. See `XXH32_round()`
[](undefined) 4819     *
[](undefined) 4820     * FIXME: Clang's output is still _much_ faster -- On an AMD Ryzen 3600,
[](undefined) 4821     * XXH3_64bits @ len=240 runs at 4.6 GB/s with Clang 9, but 3.3 GB/s on
[](undefined) 4822     * GCC 9.2, despite both emitting scalar code.
[](undefined) 4823     *
[](undefined) 4824     * GCC generates much better scalar code than Clang for the rest of XXH3,
[](undefined) 4825     * which is why finding a more optimal codepath is an interest.
[](undefined) 4826     */
[](undefined) 4827    XXH_COMPILER_GUARD(seed64);
[](undefined) 4828#endif
[](undefined) 4829    {   xxh_u64 const input_lo = XXH_readLE64(input);
[](undefined) 4830        xxh_u64 const input_hi = XXH_readLE64(input+8);
[](undefined) 4831        return XXH3_mul128_fold64(
[](undefined) 4832            input_lo ^ (XXH_readLE64(secret)   + seed64),
[](undefined) 4833            input_hi ^ (XXH_readLE64(secret+8) - seed64)
[](undefined) 4834        );
[](undefined) 4835    }
[](undefined) 4836}
[](undefined) 4837
[](undefined) 4838/* For mid range keys, XXH3 uses a Mum-hash variant. */
[](undefined) 4839XXH_FORCE_INLINE XXH_PUREF [XXH64_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/20-group__public/#ga5406b285b18dfcefa93efed489e3b603)
[](undefined) 4840XXH3_len_17to128_64b(const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT input, size_t len,
[](undefined) 4841                     const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret, size_t secretSize,
[](undefined) 4842                     [XXH64_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/20-group__public/#ga5406b285b18dfcefa93efed489e3b603) seed)
[](undefined) 4843{
[](undefined) 4844    XXH_ASSERT(secretSize >= [XXH3_SECRET_SIZE_MIN](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/14-group___x_x_h3__family/#gaeb79a9111c58af32599f8e5cd71d67d2)); (void)secretSize;
[](undefined) 4845    XXH_ASSERT(16 < len && len <= 128);
[](undefined) 4846
[](undefined) 4847    {   xxh_u64 acc = len * [XXH_PRIME64_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/17-group___x_x_h64__impl/#ga20651a9ad53cae6f0a4feb1393d5a716);
[](undefined) 4848#if XXH_SIZE_OPT >= 1
[](undefined) 4849        /* Smaller and cleaner, but slightly slower. */
[](undefined) 4850        unsigned int i = (unsigned int)(len - 1) / 32;
[](undefined) 4851        do {
[](undefined) 4852            acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+16 * i, secret+32*i, seed);
[](undefined) 4853            acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+len-16*(i+1), secret+32*i+16, seed);
[](undefined) 4854        } while (i-- != 0);
[](undefined) 4855#else
[](undefined) 4856        if (len > 32) {
[](undefined) 4857            if (len > 64) {
[](undefined) 4858                if (len > 96) {
[](undefined) 4859                    acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+48, secret+96, seed);
[](undefined) 4860                    acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+len-64, secret+112, seed);
[](undefined) 4861                }
[](undefined) 4862                acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+32, secret+64, seed);
[](undefined) 4863                acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+len-48, secret+80, seed);
[](undefined) 4864            }
[](undefined) 4865            acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+16, secret+32, seed);
[](undefined) 4866            acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+len-32, secret+48, seed);
[](undefined) 4867        }
[](undefined) 4868        acc += XXH3_mix16B(input+0, secret+0, seed);

[](undefined) 4898#if defined(__clang__)                                /* Clang */ \

[](undefined) 4900    && !defined(XXH_ENABLE_AUTOVECTORIZE)             /* Define to disable */
[](undefined) 4901        /*
[](undefined) 4902         * UGLY HACK:
[](undefined) 4903         * Clang for ARMv7-A tries to vectorize this loop, similar to GCC x86.
[](undefined) 4904         * In everywhere else, it uses scalar code.
[](undefined) 4905         *
[](undefined) 4906         * For 64->128-bit multiplies, even if the NEON was 100% optimal, it
[](undefined) 4907         * would still be slower than UMAAL (see XXH_mult64to128).
[](undefined) 4908         *
[](undefined) 4909         * Unfortunately, Clang doesn't handle the long multiplies properly and
[](undefined) 4910         * converts them to the nonexistent "vmulq_u64" intrinsic, which is then
[](undefined) 4911         * scalarized into an ugly mess of VMOV.32 instructions.
[](undefined) 4912         *
[](undefined) 4913         * This mess is difficult to avoid without turning autovectorization
[](undefined) 4914         * off completely, but they are usually relatively minor and/or not
[](undefined) 4915         * worth it to fix.
[](undefined) 4916         *
[](undefined) 4917         * This loop is the easiest to fix, as unlike XXH32, this pragma
[](undefined) 4918         * _actually works_ because it is a loop vectorization instead of an
[](undefined) 4919         * SLP vectorization.

[](undefined) 4926             */

[](undefined) 4928            acc_end += XXH3_mix16B(input+(16*i), secret+(16*(i-8)) + XXH3_MIDSIZE_STARTOFFSET, seed);
[](undefined) 4929        }
[](undefined) 4930        return XXH3_avalanche(acc + acc_end);

[](undefined) 4958/*
[](undefined) 4959 * These macros are to generate an XXH3_accumulate() function.
[](undefined) 4960 * The two arguments select the name suffix and target attribute.
[](undefined) 4961 *
[](undefined) 4962 * The name of this symbol is XXH3_accumulate_<name>() and it calls
[](undefined) 4963 * XXH3_accumulate_512_<name>().
[](undefined) 4964 *
[](undefined) 4965 * It may be useful to hand implement this function if the compiler fails to
[](undefined) 4966 * optimize the inline function.
[](undefined) 4967 */
[](undefined) 4968#define XXH3_ACCUMULATE_TEMPLATE(name)                      \
[](undefined) 4969void                                                        \
[](undefined) 4970XXH3_accumulate_##name(xxh_u64* XXH_RESTRICT acc,           \
[](undefined) 4971                       const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT input,    \
[](undefined) 4972                       const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret,   \
[](undefined) 4973                       size_t nbStripes)                    \
[](undefined) 4974{                                                           \
[](undefined) 4975    size_t n;                                               \
[](undefined) 4976    for (n = 0; n < nbStripes; n++ ) {                      \
[](undefined) 4977        const xxh_u8* const in = input + n*XXH_STRIPE_LEN;  \
[](undefined) 4978        XXH_PREFETCH(in + XXH_PREFETCH_DIST);               \
[](undefined) 4979        XXH3_accumulate_512_##name(                         \
[](undefined) 4980                 acc,                                       \
[](undefined) 4981                 in,                                        \
[](undefined) 4982                 secret + n*XXH_SECRET_CONSUME_RATE);       \
[](undefined) 4983    }                                                       \
[](undefined) 4984}

[](undefined) 4992
[](undefined) 4993/* Several intrinsic functions below are supposed to accept __int64 as argument,
[](undefined) 4994 * as documented in https://software.intel.com/sites/landingpage/IntrinsicsGuide/ .
[](undefined) 4995 * However, several environments do not define __int64 type,
[](undefined) 4996 * requiring a workaround.
[](undefined) 4997 */
[](undefined) 4998#if !defined (__VMS) \
[](undefined) 4999  && (defined (__cplusplus) \
[](undefined) 5000  || (defined (__STDC_VERSION__) && (__STDC_VERSION__ >= 199901L) /* C99 */) )
[](undefined) 5001    typedef int64_t xxh_i64;
[](undefined) 5002#else
[](undefined) 5003    /* the following type must have a width of 64-bit */
[](undefined) 5004    typedef long long xxh_i64;
[](undefined) 5005#endif
[](undefined) 5006
[](undefined) 5007
[](undefined) 5008/*
[](undefined) 5009 * XXH3_accumulate_512 is the tightest loop for long inputs, and it is the most optimized.
[](undefined) 5010 *
[](undefined) 5011 * It is a hardened version of UMAC, based off of FARSH's implementation.
[](undefined) 5012 *
[](undefined) 5013 * This was chosen because it adapts quite well to 32-bit, 64-bit, and SIMD
[](undefined) 5014 * implementations, and it is ridiculously fast.
[](undefined) 5015 *
[](undefined) 5016 * We harden it by mixing the original input to the accumulators as well as the product.
[](undefined) 5017 *
[](undefined) 5018 * This means that in the (relatively likely) case of a multiply by zero, the
[](undefined) 5019 * original input is preserved.
[](undefined) 5020 *
[](undefined) 5021 * On 128-bit inputs, we swap 64-bit pairs when we add the input to improve
[](undefined) 5022 * cross-pollination, as otherwise the upper and lower halves would be
[](undefined) 5023 * essentially independent.
[](undefined) 5024 *
[](undefined) 5025 * This doesn't matter on 64-bit hashes since they all get merged together in
[](undefined) 5026 * the end, so we skip the extra step.
[](undefined) 5027 *
[](undefined) 5028 * Both XXH3_64bits and XXH3_128bits use this subroutine.
[](undefined) 5029 */
[](undefined) 5030
[](undefined) 5031#if (XXH_VECTOR == XXH_AVX512) \
[](undefined) 5032     || (defined(XXH_DISPATCH_AVX512) && XXH_DISPATCH_AVX512 != 0)
[](undefined) 5033
[](undefined) 5034#ifndef XXH_TARGET_AVX512
[](undefined)[ 5035](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/18-group__dispatch/#ga94d06a353acaa62bbd9fbf65e865a282)# define XXH_TARGET_AVX512  /* disable attribute target */
[](undefined) 5036#endif
[](undefined) 5037
[](undefined) 5038XXH_FORCE_INLINE [XXH_TARGET_AVX512](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/18-group__dispatch/#ga94d06a353acaa62bbd9fbf65e865a282) void
[](undefined) 5039XXH3_accumulate_512_avx512(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 5040                     const void* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 5041                     const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5042{
[](undefined) 5043    __m512i* const xacc = (__m512i *) acc;
[](undefined) 5044    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 63) == 0);
[](undefined) 5045    XXH_STATIC_ASSERT(XXH_STRIPE_LEN == sizeof(__m512i));
[](undefined) 5046
[](undefined) 5047    {
[](undefined) 5048        /* data_vec    = input[0]; */
[](undefined) 5049        __m512i const data_vec    = _mm512_loadu_si512   (input);
[](undefined) 5050        /* key_vec     = secret[0]; */
[](undefined) 5051        __m512i const key_vec     = _mm512_loadu_si512   (secret);
[](undefined) 5052        /* data_key    = data_vec ^ key_vec; */
[](undefined) 5053        __m512i const data_key    = _mm512_xor_si512     (data_vec, key_vec);
[](undefined) 5054        /* data_key_lo = data_key >> 32; */
[](undefined) 5055        __m512i const data_key_lo = _mm512_srli_epi64 (data_key, 32);
[](undefined) 5056        /* product     = (data_key & 0xffffffff) * (data_key_lo & 0xffffffff); */
[](undefined) 5057        __m512i const product     = _mm512_mul_epu32     (data_key, data_key_lo);
[](undefined) 5058        /* xacc[0] += swap(data_vec); */
[](undefined) 5059        __m512i const data_swap = _mm512_shuffle_epi32(data_vec, (_MM_PERM_ENUM)_MM_SHUFFLE(1, 0, 3, 2));
[](undefined) 5060        __m512i const sum       = _mm512_add_epi64(*xacc, data_swap);
[](undefined) 5061        /* xacc[0] += product; */
[](undefined) 5062        *xacc = _mm512_add_epi64(product, sum);
[](undefined) 5063    }
[](undefined) 5064}
[](undefined) 5065XXH_FORCE_INLINE [XXH_TARGET_AVX512](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/18-group__dispatch/#ga94d06a353acaa62bbd9fbf65e865a282) XXH3_ACCUMULATE_TEMPLATE(avx512)
[](undefined) 5066
[](undefined) 5067/*
[](undefined) 5068 * XXH3_scrambleAcc: Scrambles the accumulators to improve mixing.
[](undefined) 5069 *
[](undefined) 5070 * Multiplication isn't perfect, as explained by Google in HighwayHash:
[](undefined) 5071 *
[](undefined) 5072 *  // Multiplication mixes/scrambles bytes 0-7 of the 64-bit result to
[](undefined) 5073 *  // varying degrees. In descending order of goodness, bytes
[](undefined) 5074 *  // 3 4 2 5 1 6 0 7 have quality 228 224 164 160 100 96 36 32.
[](undefined) 5075 *  // As expected, the upper and lower bytes are much worse.
[](undefined) 5076 *
[](undefined) 5077 * Source: https://github.com/google/highwayhash/blob/0aaf66b/highwayhash/hh_avx2.h#L291
[](undefined) 5078 *
[](undefined) 5079 * Since our algorithm uses a pseudorandom secret to add some variance into the
[](undefined) 5080 * mix, we don't need to (or want to) mix as often or as much as HighwayHash does.
[](undefined) 5081 *
[](undefined) 5082 * This isn't as tight as XXH3_accumulate, but still written in SIMD to avoid
[](undefined) 5083 * extraction.
[](undefined) 5084 *
[](undefined) 5085 * Both XXH3_64bits and XXH3_128bits use this subroutine.
[](undefined) 5086 */
[](undefined) 5087
[](undefined) 5088XXH_FORCE_INLINE [XXH_TARGET_AVX512](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/18-group__dispatch/#ga94d06a353acaa62bbd9fbf65e865a282) void
[](undefined) 5089XXH3_scrambleAcc_avx512(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5090{
[](undefined) 5091    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 63) == 0);
[](undefined) 5092    XXH_STATIC_ASSERT(XXH_STRIPE_LEN == sizeof(__m512i));
[](undefined) 5093    {   __m512i* const xacc = (__m512i*) acc;
[](undefined) 5094        const __m512i prime32 = _mm512_set1_epi32((int)[XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f));
[](undefined) 5095
[](undefined) 5096        /* xacc[0] ^= (xacc[0] >> 47) */
[](undefined) 5097        __m512i const acc_vec     = *xacc;
[](undefined) 5098        __m512i const shifted     = _mm512_srli_epi64    (acc_vec, 47);
[](undefined) 5099        /* xacc[0] ^= secret; */
[](undefined) 5100        __m512i const key_vec     = _mm512_loadu_si512   (secret);
[](undefined) 5101        __m512i const data_key    = _mm512_ternarylogic_epi32(key_vec, acc_vec, shifted, 0x96 /* key_vec ^ acc_vec ^ shifted */);
[](undefined) 5102
[](undefined) 5103        /* xacc[0] *= XXH_PRIME32_1; */
[](undefined) 5104        __m512i const data_key_hi = _mm512_srli_epi64 (data_key, 32);
[](undefined) 5105        __m512i const prod_lo     = _mm512_mul_epu32     (data_key, prime32);
[](undefined) 5106        __m512i const prod_hi     = _mm512_mul_epu32     (data_key_hi, prime32);
[](undefined) 5107        *xacc = _mm512_add_epi64(prod_lo, _mm512_slli_epi64(prod_hi, 32));
[](undefined) 5108    }
[](undefined) 5109}

[](undefined) 5176XXH_FORCE_INLINE [XXH_TARGET_AVX2](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/18-group__dispatch/#gad2a40b306e49c6e6240acd43484ed5e0) void
[](undefined) 5177XXH3_scrambleAcc_avx2(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5178{
[](undefined) 5179    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 31) == 0);
[](undefined) 5180    {   __m256i* const xacc = (__m256i*) acc;
[](undefined) 5181        /* Unaligned. This is mainly for pointer arithmetic, and because
[](undefined) 5182         * _mm256_loadu_si256 requires a const __m256i * pointer for some reason. */
[](undefined) 5183        const         __m256i* const xsecret = (const __m256i *) secret;
[](undefined) 5184        const __m256i prime32 = _mm256_set1_epi32((int)[XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f));
[](undefined) 5185
[](undefined) 5186        size_t i;
[](undefined) 5187        for (i=0; i < XXH_STRIPE_LEN/sizeof(__m256i); i++) {
[](undefined) 5188            /* xacc[i] ^= (xacc[i] >> 47) */
[](undefined) 5189            __m256i const acc_vec     = xacc[i];
[](undefined) 5190            __m256i const shifted     = _mm256_srli_epi64    (acc_vec, 47);
[](undefined) 5191            __m256i const data_vec    = _mm256_xor_si256     (acc_vec, shifted);
[](undefined) 5192            /* xacc[i] ^= xsecret; */
[](undefined) 5193            __m256i const key_vec     = _mm256_loadu_si256   (xsecret+i);
[](undefined) 5194            __m256i const data_key    = _mm256_xor_si256     (data_vec, key_vec);
[](undefined) 5195
[](undefined) 5196            /* xacc[i] *= XXH_PRIME32_1; */
[](undefined) 5197            __m256i const data_key_hi = _mm256_srli_epi64 (data_key, 32);

[](undefined) 5260        const         __m128i* const xsecret = (const __m128i *) secret;

[](undefined) 5263        for (i=0; i < XXH_STRIPE_LEN/sizeof(__m128i); i++) {

[](undefined) 5389
[](undefined) 5390XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5391XXH3_accumulate_512_neon( void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 5392                    const void* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 5393                    const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5394{
[](undefined) 5395    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 15) == 0);
[](undefined) 5396    XXH_STATIC_ASSERT([XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c) > 0 && [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c) <= XXH_ACC_NB && [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c) % 2 == 0);
[](undefined) 5397    {   /* GCC for darwin arm64 does not like aliasing here */
[](undefined) 5398        xxh_aliasing_uint64x2_t* const xacc = (xxh_aliasing_uint64x2_t*) acc;
[](undefined) 5399        /* We don't use a uint32x4_t pointer because it causes bus errors on ARMv7. */
[](undefined) 5400        uint8_t const* xinput = (const uint8_t *) input;
[](undefined) 5401        uint8_t const* xsecret  = (const uint8_t *) secret;
[](undefined) 5402
[](undefined) 5403        size_t i;
[](undefined) 5404#ifdef __wasm_simd128__
[](undefined) 5405        /*
[](undefined) 5406         * On WASM SIMD128, Clang emits direct address loads when XXH3_kSecret
[](undefined) 5407         * is constant propagated, which results in it converting it to this
[](undefined) 5408         * inside the loop:
[](undefined) 5409         *
[](undefined) 5410         *    a = v128.load(XXH3_kSecret +  0 + $secret_offset, offset = 0)
[](undefined) 5411         *    b = v128.load(XXH3_kSecret + 16 + $secret_offset, offset = 0)
[](undefined) 5412         *    ...
[](undefined) 5413         *
[](undefined) 5414         * This requires a full 32-bit address immediate (and therefore a 6 byte
[](undefined) 5415         * instruction) as well as an add for each offset.
[](undefined) 5416         *
[](undefined) 5417         * Putting an asm guard prevents it from folding (at the cost of losing
[](undefined) 5418         * the alignment hint), and uses the free offset in `v128.load` instead
[](undefined) 5419         * of adding secret_offset each time which overall reduces code size by
[](undefined) 5420         * about a kilobyte and improves performance.
[](undefined) 5421         */
[](undefined) 5422        XXH_COMPILER_GUARD(xsecret);
[](undefined) 5423#endif
[](undefined) 5424        /* Scalar lanes use the normal scalarRound routine */
[](undefined) 5425        for (i = [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c); i < XXH_ACC_NB; i++) {
[](undefined) 5426            XXH3_scalarRound(acc, input, secret, i);
[](undefined) 5427        }
[](undefined) 5428        i = 0;
[](undefined) 5429        /* 4 NEON lanes at a time. */
[](undefined) 5430        for (; i+1 < [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c) / 2; i+=2) {
[](undefined) 5431            /* data_vec = xinput[i]; */
[](undefined) 5432            uint64x2_t data_vec_1 = XXH_vld1q_u64(xinput  + (i * 16));
[](undefined) 5433            uint64x2_t data_vec_2 = XXH_vld1q_u64(xinput  + ((i+1) * 16));
[](undefined) 5434            /* key_vec  = xsecret[i];  */
[](undefined) 5435            uint64x2_t key_vec_1  = XXH_vld1q_u64(xsecret + (i * 16));
[](undefined) 5436            uint64x2_t key_vec_2  = XXH_vld1q_u64(xsecret + ((i+1) * 16));
[](undefined) 5437            /* data_swap = swap(data_vec) */
[](undefined) 5438            uint64x2_t data_swap_1 = vextq_u64(data_vec_1, data_vec_1, 1);
[](undefined) 5439            uint64x2_t data_swap_2 = vextq_u64(data_vec_2, data_vec_2, 1);
[](undefined) 5440            /* data_key = data_vec ^ key_vec; */
[](undefined) 5441            uint64x2_t data_key_1 = veorq_u64(data_vec_1, key_vec_1);
[](undefined) 5442            uint64x2_t data_key_2 = veorq_u64(data_vec_2, key_vec_2);
[](undefined) 5443
[](undefined) 5444            /*
[](undefined) 5445             * If we reinterpret the 64x2 vectors as 32x4 vectors, we can use a
[](undefined) 5446             * de-interleave operation for 4 lanes in 1 step with `vuzpq_u32` to
[](undefined) 5447             * get one vector with the low 32 bits of each lane, and one vector
[](undefined) 5448             * with the high 32 bits of each lane.
[](undefined) 5449             *
[](undefined) 5450             * The intrinsic returns a double vector because the original ARMv7-a
[](undefined) 5451             * instruction modified both arguments in place. AArch64 and SIMD128 emit
[](undefined) 5452             * two instructions from this intrinsic.
[](undefined) 5453             *
[](undefined) 5454             *  [ dk11L | dk11H | dk12L | dk12H ] -> [ dk11L | dk12L | dk21L | dk22L ]
[](undefined) 5455             *  [ dk21L | dk21H | dk22L | dk22H ] -> [ dk11H | dk12H | dk21H | dk22H ]
[](undefined) 5456             */
[](undefined) 5457            uint32x4x2_t unzipped = vuzpq_u32(
[](undefined) 5458                vreinterpretq_u32_u64(data_key_1),
[](undefined) 5459                vreinterpretq_u32_u64(data_key_2)
[](undefined) 5460            );
[](undefined) 5461            /* data_key_lo = data_key & 0xFFFFFFFF */
[](undefined) 5462            uint32x4_t data_key_lo = unzipped.val[0];
[](undefined) 5463            /* data_key_hi = data_key >> 32 */
[](undefined) 5464            uint32x4_t data_key_hi = unzipped.val[1];
[](undefined) 5465            /*
[](undefined) 5466             * Then, we can split the vectors horizontally and multiply which, as for most
[](undefined) 5467             * widening intrinsics, have a variant that works on both high half vectors
[](undefined) 5468             * for free on AArch64. A similar instruction is available on SIMD128.
[](undefined) 5469             *
[](undefined) 5470             * sum = data_swap + (u64x2) data_key_lo * (u64x2) data_key_hi
[](undefined) 5471             */
[](undefined) 5472            uint64x2_t sum_1 = XXH_vmlal_low_u32(data_swap_1, data_key_lo, data_key_hi);
[](undefined) 5473            uint64x2_t sum_2 = XXH_vmlal_high_u32(data_swap_2, data_key_lo, data_key_hi);
[](undefined) 5474            /*
[](undefined) 5475             * Clang reorders
[](undefined) 5476             *    a += b * c;     // umlal   swap.2d, dkl.2s, dkh.2s
[](undefined) 5477             *    c += a;         // add     acc.2d, acc.2d, swap.2d
[](undefined) 5478             * to
[](undefined) 5479             *    c += a;         // add     acc.2d, acc.2d, swap.2d
[](undefined) 5480             *    c += b * c;     // umlal   acc.2d, dkl.2s, dkh.2s
[](undefined) 5481             *
[](undefined) 5482             * While it would make sense in theory since the addition is faster,
[](undefined) 5483             * for reasons likely related to umlal being limited to certain NEON
[](undefined) 5484             * pipelines, this is worse. A compiler guard fixes this.
[](undefined) 5485             */
[](undefined) 5486            XXH_COMPILER_GUARD_CLANG_NEON(sum_1);
[](undefined) 5487            XXH_COMPILER_GUARD_CLANG_NEON(sum_2);
[](undefined) 5488            /* xacc[i] = acc_vec + sum; */
[](undefined) 5489            xacc[i]   = vaddq_u64(xacc[i], sum_1);
[](undefined) 5490            xacc[i+1] = vaddq_u64(xacc[i+1], sum_2);
[](undefined) 5491        }
[](undefined) 5492        /* Operate on the remaining NEON lanes 2 at a time. */
[](undefined) 5493        for (; i < [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c) / 2; i++) {
[](undefined) 5494            /* data_vec = xinput[i]; */
[](undefined) 5495            uint64x2_t data_vec = XXH_vld1q_u64(xinput  + (i * 16));
[](undefined) 5496            /* key_vec  = xsecret[i];  */
[](undefined) 5497            uint64x2_t key_vec  = XXH_vld1q_u64(xsecret + (i * 16));
[](undefined) 5498            /* acc_vec_2 = swap(data_vec) */
[](undefined) 5499            uint64x2_t data_swap = vextq_u64(data_vec, data_vec, 1);
[](undefined) 5500            /* data_key = data_vec ^ key_vec; */
[](undefined) 5501            uint64x2_t data_key = veorq_u64(data_vec, key_vec);
[](undefined) 5502            /* For two lanes, just use VMOVN and VSHRN. */
[](undefined) 5503            /* data_key_lo = data_key & 0xFFFFFFFF; */
[](undefined) 5504            uint32x2_t data_key_lo = vmovn_u64(data_key);
[](undefined) 5505            /* data_key_hi = data_key >> 32; */
[](undefined) 5506            uint32x2_t data_key_hi = vshrn_n_u64(data_key, 32);
[](undefined) 5507            /* sum = data_swap + (u64x2) data_key_lo * (u64x2) data_key_hi; */
[](undefined) 5508            uint64x2_t sum = vmlal_u32(data_swap, data_key_lo, data_key_hi);
[](undefined) 5509            /* Same Clang workaround as before */
[](undefined) 5510            XXH_COMPILER_GUARD_CLANG_NEON(sum);
[](undefined) 5511            /* xacc[i] = acc_vec + sum; */
[](undefined) 5512            xacc[i] = vaddq_u64 (xacc[i], sum);
[](undefined) 5513        }
[](undefined) 5514    }
[](undefined) 5515}

[](undefined) 5518XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5519XXH3_scrambleAcc_neon(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)

[](undefined) 5521    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 15) == 0);
[](undefined) 5522
[](undefined) 5523    {   xxh_aliasing_uint64x2_t* xacc       = (xxh_aliasing_uint64x2_t*) acc;
[](undefined) 5524        uint8_t const* xsecret = (uint8_t const*) secret;
[](undefined) 5525
[](undefined) 5526        size_t i;
[](undefined) 5527        /* WASM uses operator overloads and doesn't need these. */
[](undefined) 5528#ifndef __wasm_simd128__
[](undefined) 5529        /* { prime32_1, prime32_1 } */
[](undefined) 5530        uint32x2_t const kPrimeLo = vdup_n_u32([XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f));
[](undefined) 5531        /* { 0, prime32_1, 0, prime32_1 } */
[](undefined) 5532        uint32x4_t const kPrimeHi = vreinterpretq_u32_u64(vdupq_n_u64((xxh_u64)[XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f) << 32));
[](undefined) 5533#endif
[](undefined) 5534
[](undefined) 5535        /* AArch64 uses both scalar and neon at the same time */
[](undefined) 5536        for (i = [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c); i < XXH_ACC_NB; i++) {
[](undefined) 5537            XXH3_scalarScrambleRound(acc, secret, i);
[](undefined) 5538        }
[](undefined) 5539        for (i=0; i < [XXH3_NEON_LANES](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#gac799ce966f18e4837f7fdc3d51917e9c) / 2; i++) {
[](undefined) 5540            /* xacc[i] ^= (xacc[i] >> 47); */
[](undefined) 5541            uint64x2_t acc_vec  = xacc[i];
[](undefined) 5542            uint64x2_t shifted  = vshrq_n_u64(acc_vec, 47);
[](undefined) 5543            uint64x2_t data_vec = veorq_u64(acc_vec, shifted);
[](undefined) 5544
[](undefined) 5545            /* xacc[i] ^= xsecret[i]; */
[](undefined) 5546            uint64x2_t key_vec  = XXH_vld1q_u64(xsecret + (i * 16));
[](undefined) 5547            uint64x2_t data_key = veorq_u64(data_vec, key_vec);
[](undefined) 5548            /* xacc[i] *= XXH_PRIME32_1 */
[](undefined) 5549#ifdef __wasm_simd128__
[](undefined) 5550            /* SIMD128 has multiply by u64x2, use it instead of expanding and scalarizing */
[](undefined) 5551            xacc[i] = data_key * [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f);
[](undefined) 5552#else
[](undefined) 5553            /*
[](undefined) 5554             * Expanded version with portable NEON intrinsics
[](undefined) 5555             *
[](undefined) 5556             *    lo(x) * lo(y) + (hi(x) * lo(y) << 32)
[](undefined) 5557             *
[](undefined) 5558             * prod_hi = hi(data_key) * lo(prime) << 32
[](undefined) 5559             *
[](undefined) 5560             * Since we only need 32 bits of this multiply a trick can be used, reinterpreting the vector
[](undefined) 5561             * as a uint32x4_t and multiplying by { 0, prime, 0, prime } to cancel out the unwanted bits
[](undefined) 5562             * and avoid the shift.
[](undefined) 5563             */
[](undefined) 5564            uint32x4_t prod_hi = vmulq_u32 (vreinterpretq_u32_u64(data_key), kPrimeHi);
[](undefined) 5565            /* Extract low bits for vmlal_u32  */
[](undefined) 5566            uint32x2_t data_key_lo = vmovn_u64(data_key);
[](undefined) 5567            /* xacc[i] = prod_hi + lo(data_key) * XXH_PRIME32_1; */
[](undefined) 5568            xacc[i] = vmlal_u32(vreinterpretq_u64_u32(prod_hi), data_key_lo, kPrimeLo);
[](undefined) 5569#endif
[](undefined) 5570        }
[](undefined) 5571    }
[](undefined) 5572}
[](undefined) 5573#endif

[](undefined) 5575#if (XXH_VECTOR == XXH_VSX)
[](undefined) 5576
[](undefined) 5577XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5578XXH3_accumulate_512_vsx(  void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 5579                    const void* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 5580                    const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5581{
[](undefined) 5582    /* presumed aligned */
[](undefined) 5583    xxh_aliasing_u64x2* const xacc = (xxh_aliasing_u64x2*) acc;
[](undefined) 5584    xxh_u8 const* const xinput   = (xxh_u8 const*) input;   /* no alignment restriction */
[](undefined) 5585    xxh_u8 const* const xsecret  = (xxh_u8 const*) secret;    /* no alignment restriction */
[](undefined) 5586    xxh_u64x2 const v32 = { 32, 32 };
[](undefined) 5587    size_t i;
[](undefined) 5588    for (i = 0; i < XXH_STRIPE_LEN / sizeof(xxh_u64x2); i++) {
[](undefined) 5589        /* data_vec = xinput[i]; */
[](undefined) 5590        xxh_u64x2 const data_vec = XXH_vec_loadu(xinput + 16*i);
[](undefined) 5591        /* key_vec = xsecret[i]; */
[](undefined) 5592        xxh_u64x2 const key_vec  = XXH_vec_loadu(xsecret + 16*i);
[](undefined) 5593        xxh_u64x2 const data_key = data_vec ^ key_vec;
[](undefined) 5594        /* shuffled = (data_key << 32) | (data_key >> 32); */
[](undefined) 5595        xxh_u32x4 const shuffled = (xxh_u32x4)vec_rl(data_key, v32);
[](undefined) 5596        /* product = ((xxh_u64x2)data_key & 0xFFFFFFFF) * ((xxh_u64x2)shuffled & 0xFFFFFFFF); */
[](undefined) 5597        xxh_u64x2 const product  = XXH_vec_mulo((xxh_u32x4)data_key, shuffled);
[](undefined) 5598        /* acc_vec = xacc[i]; */
[](undefined) 5599        xxh_u64x2 acc_vec        = xacc[i];
[](undefined) 5600        acc_vec += product;
[](undefined) 5601
[](undefined) 5602        /* swap high and low halves */
[](undefined) 5603#ifdef __s390x__
[](undefined) 5604        acc_vec += vec_permi(data_vec, data_vec, 2);
[](undefined) 5605#else
[](undefined) 5606        acc_vec += vec_xxpermdi(data_vec, data_vec, 2);
[](undefined) 5607#endif
[](undefined) 5608        xacc[i] = acc_vec;
[](undefined) 5609    }
[](undefined) 5610}

[](undefined) 5613XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5614XXH3_scrambleAcc_vsx(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5615{
[](undefined) 5616    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 15) == 0);
[](undefined) 5617
[](undefined) 5618    {   xxh_aliasing_u64x2* const xacc = (xxh_aliasing_u64x2*) acc;
[](undefined) 5619        const xxh_u8* const xsecret = (const xxh_u8*) secret;
[](undefined) 5620        /* constants */
[](undefined) 5621        xxh_u64x2 const v32  = { 32, 32 };
[](undefined) 5622        xxh_u64x2 const v47 = { 47, 47 };
[](undefined) 5623        xxh_u32x4 const prime = { [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f), [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f), [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f), [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f) };
[](undefined) 5624        size_t i;
[](undefined) 5625        for (i = 0; i < XXH_STRIPE_LEN / sizeof(xxh_u64x2); i++) {
[](undefined) 5626            /* xacc[i] ^= (xacc[i] >> 47); */
[](undefined) 5627            xxh_u64x2 const acc_vec  = xacc[i];
[](undefined) 5628            xxh_u64x2 const data_vec = acc_vec ^ (acc_vec >> v47);
[](undefined) 5629
[](undefined) 5630            /* xacc[i] ^= xsecret[i]; */
[](undefined) 5631            xxh_u64x2 const key_vec  = XXH_vec_loadu(xsecret + 16*i);
[](undefined) 5632            xxh_u64x2 const data_key = data_vec ^ key_vec;
[](undefined) 5633
[](undefined) 5634            /* xacc[i] *= XXH_PRIME32_1 */
[](undefined) 5635            /* prod_lo = ((xxh_u64x2)data_key & 0xFFFFFFFF) * ((xxh_u64x2)prime & 0xFFFFFFFF);  */
[](undefined) 5636            xxh_u64x2 const prod_even  = XXH_vec_mule((xxh_u32x4)data_key, prime);
[](undefined) 5637            /* prod_hi = ((xxh_u64x2)data_key >> 32) * ((xxh_u64x2)prime >> 32);  */
[](undefined) 5638            xxh_u64x2 const prod_odd  = XXH_vec_mulo((xxh_u32x4)data_key, prime);
[](undefined) 5639            xacc[i] = prod_odd + (prod_even << v32);
[](undefined) 5640    }   }

[](undefined) 5860        __m256i* const xacc = (__m256i*) acc;
[](undefined) 5861        const __m256i* const xsecret = (const __m256i *) secret;
[](undefined) 5862        const __m256i prime32 = __lasx_xvreplgr2vr_d([XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f));
[](undefined) 5863        size_t i;
[](undefined) 5864
[](undefined) 5865        for (i = 0; i < XXH_STRIPE_LEN / sizeof(__m256i); i++) {
[](undefined) 5866            /* xacc[i] ^= (xacc[i] >> 47) */
[](undefined) 5867            __m256i const acc_vec = xacc[i];
[](undefined) 5868            __m256i const shifted = __lasx_xvsrli_d(acc_vec, 47);
[](undefined) 5869            __m256i const data_vec = __lasx_xvxor_v(acc_vec, shifted);
[](undefined) 5870            /* xacc[i] ^= xsecret[i]; */
[](undefined) 5871            __m256i const key_vec = __lasx_xvld(xsecret + i, 0);
[](undefined) 5872            __m256i const data_key = __lasx_xvxor_v(data_vec, key_vec);
[](undefined) 5873
[](undefined) 5874            /* xacc[i] *= XXH_PRIME32_1; */
[](undefined) 5875            xacc[i] = __lasx_xvmul_d(data_key, prime32);
[](undefined) 5876        }
[](undefined) 5877    }
[](undefined) 5878}
[](undefined) 5879
[](undefined) 5880#endif
[](undefined) 5881
[](undefined) 5882#if (XXH_VECTOR == XXH_RVV)
[](undefined) 5883    #define XXH_CONCAT2(X, Y) X ## Y
[](undefined) 5884    #define XXH_CONCAT(X, Y) XXH_CONCAT2(X, Y)
[](undefined) 5885#if ((defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__) && __GNUC__ < 13) || \
[](undefined) 5886        (defined(__clang__) && __clang_major__ < 16))
[](undefined) 5887    #define XXH_RVOP(op) op
[](undefined) 5888    #define XXH_RVCAST(op) XXH_CONCAT(vreinterpret_v_, op)
[](undefined) 5889#else
[](undefined) 5890    #define XXH_RVOP(op) XXH_CONCAT(__riscv_, op)
[](undefined) 5891    #define XXH_RVCAST(op) XXH_CONCAT(__riscv_vreinterpret_v_, op)
[](undefined) 5892#endif
[](undefined) 5893XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5894XXH3_accumulate_512_rvv(  void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 5895                    const void* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 5896                    const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5897{
[](undefined) 5898    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 63) == 0);
[](undefined) 5899    {
[](undefined) 5900        // Try to set vector lenght to 512 bits.
[](undefined) 5901        // If this length is unavailable, then maximum available will be used
[](undefined) 5902        size_t vl = XXH_RVOP(vsetvl_e64m2)(8);
[](undefined) 5903
[](undefined) 5904        uint64_t*       xacc    = (uint64_t*) acc;
[](undefined) 5905        const uint64_t* xinput  = (const uint64_t*) input;
[](undefined) 5906        const uint64_t* xsecret = (const uint64_t*) secret;
[](undefined) 5907        static const uint64_t swap_mask[16] = {1, 0, 3, 2, 5, 4, 7, 6, 9, 8, 11, 10, 13, 12, 15, 14};
[](undefined) 5908        vuint64m2_t xswap_mask = XXH_RVOP(vle64_v_u64m2)(swap_mask, vl);
[](undefined) 5909
[](undefined) 5910        size_t i;
[](undefined) 5911        for (i = 0; i < XXH_STRIPE_LEN/8; i += vl) {
[](undefined) 5912            /* data_vec = xinput[i]; */
[](undefined) 5913            vuint64m2_t data_vec = XXH_RVCAST(u8m2_u64m2)(XXH_RVOP(vle8_v_u8m2)((const uint8_t*)(xinput + i), vl * 8));
[](undefined) 5914            /* key_vec = xsecret[i]; */
[](undefined) 5915            vuint64m2_t key_vec = XXH_RVCAST(u8m2_u64m2)(XXH_RVOP(vle8_v_u8m2)((const uint8_t*)(xsecret + i), vl * 8));
[](undefined) 5916            /* acc_vec = xacc[i]; */
[](undefined) 5917            vuint64m2_t acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vle64_v_u64m2)(xacc + i, vl);
[](undefined) 5918            /* data_key = data_vec ^ key_vec; */
[](undefined) 5919            vuint64m2_t data_key = XXH_RVOP(vxor_vv_u64m2)(data_vec, key_vec, vl);
[](undefined) 5920            /* data_key_hi = data_key >> 32; */
[](undefined) 5921            vuint64m2_t data_key_hi = XXH_RVOP(vsrl_vx_u64m2)(data_key, 32, vl);
[](undefined) 5922            /* data_key_lo = data_key & 0xffffffff; */
[](undefined) 5923            vuint64m2_t data_key_lo = XXH_RVOP(vand_vx_u64m2)(data_key, 0xffffffff, vl);
[](undefined) 5924            /* swap high and low halves */
[](undefined) 5925            vuint64m2_t data_swap = XXH_RVOP(vrgather_vv_u64m2)(data_vec, xswap_mask, vl);
[](undefined) 5926            /* acc_vec += data_key_lo * data_key_hi; */
[](undefined) 5927            acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vmacc_vv_u64m2)(acc_vec, data_key_lo, data_key_hi, vl);
[](undefined) 5928            /* acc_vec += data_swap; */
[](undefined) 5929            acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vadd_vv_u64m2)(acc_vec, data_swap, vl);
[](undefined) 5930            /* xacc[i] = acc_vec; */
[](undefined) 5931            XXH_RVOP(vse64_v_u64m2)(xacc + i, acc_vec, vl);
[](undefined) 5932        }
[](undefined) 5933    }
[](undefined) 5934}
[](undefined) 5935

[](undefined) 5938XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5939XXH3_scrambleAcc_rvv(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 5940{
[](undefined) 5941    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & 15) == 0);
[](undefined) 5942    {
[](undefined) 5943        size_t count = XXH_STRIPE_LEN/8;
[](undefined) 5944        uint64_t* xacc = (uint64_t*)acc;
[](undefined) 5945        const uint8_t* xsecret = (const uint8_t *)secret;
[](undefined) 5946        size_t vl;
[](undefined) 5947        for (; count > 0; count -= vl, xacc += vl, xsecret += vl*8) {
[](undefined) 5948            vl = XXH_RVOP(vsetvl_e64m2)(count);
[](undefined) 5949            {
[](undefined) 5950                /* key_vec = xsecret[i]; */
[](undefined) 5951                vuint64m2_t key_vec = XXH_RVCAST(u8m2_u64m2)(XXH_RVOP(vle8_v_u8m2)(xsecret, vl*8));
[](undefined) 5952                /* acc_vec = xacc[i]; */
[](undefined) 5953                vuint64m2_t acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vle64_v_u64m2)(xacc, vl);
[](undefined) 5954                /* acc_vec ^= acc_vec >> 47; */
[](undefined) 5955                vuint64m2_t vsrl = XXH_RVOP(vsrl_vx_u64m2)(acc_vec, 47, vl);
[](undefined) 5956                acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vxor_vv_u64m2)(acc_vec, vsrl, vl);
[](undefined) 5957                /* acc_vec ^= key_vec; */
[](undefined) 5958                acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vxor_vv_u64m2)(acc_vec, key_vec, vl);
[](undefined) 5959                /* acc_vec *= XXH_PRIME32_1; */
[](undefined) 5960                acc_vec = XXH_RVOP(vmul_vx_u64m2)(acc_vec, [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f), vl);
[](undefined) 5961                /* xacc[i] *= acc_vec; */
[](undefined) 5962                XXH_RVOP(vse64_v_u64m2)(xacc, acc_vec, vl);
[](undefined) 5963            }
[](undefined) 5964        }
[](undefined) 5965    }
[](undefined) 5966}

[](undefined) 5968XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 5969XXH3_initCustomSecret_rvv(void* XXH_RESTRICT customSecret, xxh_u64 seed64)
[](undefined) 5970{
[](undefined) 5971    XXH_STATIC_ASSERT(XXH_SEC_ALIGN >= 8);
[](undefined) 5972    XXH_ASSERT(((size_t)customSecret & 7) == 0);
[](undefined) 5973    (void)(&XXH_writeLE64);
[](undefined) 5974    {
[](undefined) 5975        size_t count = XXH_SECRET_DEFAULT_SIZE/8;
[](undefined) 5976        size_t vl;
[](undefined) 5977        size_t VLMAX = XXH_RVOP(vsetvlmax_e64m2)();
[](undefined) 5978        int64_t* cSecret = (int64_t*)customSecret;
[](undefined) 5979        const int64_t* kSecret = (const int64_t*)(const void*)XXH3_kSecret;
[](undefined) 5980
[](undefined) 5981#if __riscv_v_intrinsic >= 1000000
[](undefined) 5982        // ratified v1.0 intrinics version
[](undefined) 5983        vbool32_t mneg = XXH_RVCAST(u8m1_b32)(
[](undefined) 5984                         XXH_RVOP(vmv_v_x_u8m1)(0xaa, XXH_RVOP(vsetvlmax_e8m1)()));
[](undefined) 5985#else
[](undefined) 5986        // support pre-ratification intrinics, which lack mask to vector casts
[](undefined) 5987        size_t vlmax = XXH_RVOP(vsetvlmax_e8m1)();
[](undefined) 5988        vbool32_t mneg = XXH_RVOP(vmseq_vx_u8mf4_b32)(
[](undefined) 5989                         XXH_RVOP(vand_vx_u8mf4)(
[](undefined) 5990                         XXH_RVOP(vid_v_u8mf4)(vlmax), 1, vlmax), 1, vlmax);
[](undefined) 5991#endif
[](undefined) 5992        vint64m2_t seed = XXH_RVOP(vmv_v_x_i64m2)((int64_t)seed64, VLMAX);
[](undefined) 5993        seed = XXH_RVOP(vneg_v_i64m2_mu)(mneg, seed, seed, VLMAX);
[](undefined) 5994
[](undefined) 5995        for (; count > 0; count -= vl, cSecret += vl, kSecret += vl) {
[](undefined) 5996            /* make sure vl=VLMAX until last iteration */
[](undefined) 5997            vl = XXH_RVOP(vsetvl_e64m2)(count < VLMAX ? count : VLMAX);
[](undefined) 5998            {
[](undefined) 5999                vint64m2_t src = XXH_RVOP(vle64_v_i64m2)(kSecret, vl);
[](undefined) 6000                vint64m2_t res = XXH_RVOP(vadd_vv_i64m2)(src, seed, vl);
[](undefined) 6001                XXH_RVOP(vse64_v_i64m2)(cSecret, res, vl);
[](undefined) 6002            }
[](undefined) 6003        }
[](undefined) 6004    }
[](undefined) 6005}
[](undefined) 6006#endif

[](undefined) 6009/* scalar variants - universal */
[](undefined) 6010
[](undefined) 6011#if defined(__aarch64__) && (defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__))
[](undefined) 6012/*
[](undefined) 6013 * In XXH3_scalarRound(), GCC and Clang have a similar codegen issue, where they
[](undefined) 6014 * emit an excess mask and a full 64-bit multiply-add (MADD X-form).
[](undefined) 6015 *
[](undefined) 6016 * While this might not seem like much, as AArch64 is a 64-bit architecture, only
[](undefined) 6017 * big Cortex designs have a full 64-bit multiplier.
[](undefined) 6018 *
[](undefined) 6019 * On the little cores, the smaller 32-bit multiplier is used, and full 64-bit
[](undefined) 6020 * multiplies expand to 2-3 multiplies in microcode. This has a major penalty
[](undefined) 6021 * of up to 4 latency cycles and 2 stall cycles in the multiply pipeline.
[](undefined) 6022 *
[](undefined) 6023 * Thankfully, AArch64 still provides the 32-bit long multiply-add (UMADDL) which does
[](undefined) 6024 * not have this penalty and does the mask automatically.
[](undefined) 6025 */
[](undefined) 6026XXH_FORCE_INLINE xxh_u64
[](undefined) 6027XXH_mult32to64_add64(xxh_u64 lhs, xxh_u64 rhs, xxh_u64 acc)
[](undefined) 6028{
[](undefined) 6029    xxh_u64 ret;
[](undefined) 6030    /* note: %x = 64-bit register, %w = 32-bit register */
[](undefined) 6031    __asm__("umaddl %x0, %w1, %w2, %x3" : "=r" (ret) : "r" (lhs), "r" (rhs), "r" (acc));
[](undefined) 6032    return ret;
[](undefined) 6033}
[](undefined) 6034#else
[](undefined) 6035XXH_FORCE_INLINE xxh_u64
[](undefined) 6036XXH_mult32to64_add64(xxh_u64 lhs, xxh_u64 rhs, xxh_u64 acc)
[](undefined) 6037{
[](undefined) 6038    return XXH_mult32to64((xxh_u32)lhs, (xxh_u32)rhs) + acc;
[](undefined) 6039}
[](undefined) 6040#endif
[](undefined) 6041
[](undefined) 6049XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 6050XXH3_scalarRound(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 6051                 void const* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 6052                 void const* XXH_RESTRICT secret,
[](undefined) 6053                 size_t lane)
[](undefined) 6054{
[](undefined) 6055    xxh_u64* xacc = (xxh_u64*) acc;
[](undefined) 6056    xxh_u8 const* xinput  = (xxh_u8 const*) input;
[](undefined) 6057    xxh_u8 const* xsecret = (xxh_u8 const*) secret;
[](undefined) 6058    XXH_ASSERT(lane < XXH_ACC_NB);
[](undefined) 6059    XXH_ASSERT(((size_t)acc & ([XXH_ACC_ALIGN](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#ga15f2f40c9206c691f66cf260841f8ad1)-1)) == 0);
[](undefined) 6060    {
[](undefined) 6061        xxh_u64 const data_val = XXH_readLE64(xinput + lane * 8);
[](undefined) 6062        xxh_u64 const data_key = data_val ^ XXH_readLE64(xsecret + lane * 8);
[](undefined) 6063        xacc[lane ^ 1] += data_val; /* swap adjacent lanes */
[](undefined) 6064        xacc[lane] = XXH_mult32to64_add64(data_key /* & 0xFFFFFFFF */, data_key >> 32, xacc[lane]);
[](undefined) 6065    }
[](undefined) 6066}

[](undefined) 6072XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 6073XXH3_accumulate_512_scalar(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 6074                     const void* XXH_RESTRICT input,
[](undefined) 6075                     const void* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 6076{
[](undefined) 6077    size_t i;
[](undefined) 6078    /* ARM GCC refuses to unroll this loop, resulting in a 24% slowdown on ARMv6. */
[](undefined) 6079#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__) && __GNUC__ >= 8 \
[](undefined) 6080  && (defined(__arm__) || defined(__thumb2__)) \
[](undefined) 6081  && defined(__ARM_FEATURE_UNALIGNED) /* no unaligned access just wastes bytes */ \
[](undefined) 6082  && XXH_SIZE_OPT <= 0
[](undefined) 6083#  pragma GCC unroll 8
[](undefined) 6084#endif
[](undefined) 6085    for (i=0; i < XXH_ACC_NB; i++) {
[](undefined) 6086        XXH3_scalarRound(acc, input, secret, i);
[](undefined) 6087    }
[](undefined) 6088}

[](undefined) 6098XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 6099XXH3_scalarScrambleRound(void* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 6100                         void const* XXH_RESTRICT secret,
[](undefined) 6101                         size_t lane)
[](undefined) 6102{
[](undefined) 6103    xxh_u64* const xacc = (xxh_u64*) acc;   /* presumed aligned */
[](undefined) 6104    const xxh_u8* const xsecret = (const xxh_u8*) secret;   /* no alignment restriction */
[](undefined) 6105    XXH_ASSERT((((size_t)acc) & ([XXH_ACC_ALIGN](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/21-group__tuning/#ga15f2f40c9206c691f66cf260841f8ad1)-1)) == 0);
[](undefined) 6106    XXH_ASSERT(lane < XXH_ACC_NB);
[](undefined) 6107    {
[](undefined) 6108        xxh_u64 const key64 = XXH_readLE64(xsecret + lane * 8);
[](undefined) 6109        xxh_u64 acc64 = xacc[lane];
[](undefined) 6110        acc64 = XXH_xorshift64(acc64, 47);
[](undefined) 6111        acc64 ^= key64;
[](undefined) 6112        acc64 *= [XXH_PRIME32_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga29fede2b86ee758448c55edf6171e11f);
[](undefined) 6113        xacc[lane] = acc64;
[](undefined) 6114    }

[](undefined) 6130XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 6131XXH3_initCustomSecret_scalar(void* XXH_RESTRICT customSecret, xxh_u64 seed64)
[](undefined) 6132{
[](undefined) 6133    /*
[](undefined) 6134     * We need a separate pointer for the hack below,
[](undefined) 6135     * which requires a non-const pointer.
[](undefined) 6136     * Any decent compiler will optimize this out otherwise.
[](undefined) 6137     */
[](undefined) 6138    const xxh_u8* kSecretPtr = XXH3_kSecret;
[](undefined) 6139    XXH_STATIC_ASSERT((XXH_SECRET_DEFAULT_SIZE & 15) == 0);
[](undefined) 6140
[](undefined) 6141#if defined(__GNUC__) && defined(__aarch64__)
[](undefined) 6142    /*
[](undefined) 6143     * UGLY HACK:
[](undefined) 6144     * GCC and Clang generate a bunch of MOV/MOVK pairs for aarch64, and they are
[](undefined) 6145     * placed sequentially, in order, at the top of the unrolled loop.
[](undefined) 6146     *
[](undefined) 6147     * While MOVK is great for generating constants (2 cycles for a 64-bit
[](undefined) 6148     * constant compared to 4 cycles for LDR), it fights for bandwidth with
[](undefined) 6149     * the arithmetic instructions.
[](undefined) 6150     *
[](undefined) 6151     *   I   L   S
[](undefined) 6152     * MOVK
[](undefined) 6153     * MOVK
[](undefined) 6154     * MOVK
[](undefined) 6155     * MOVK
[](undefined) 6156     * ADD
[](undefined) 6157     * SUB      STR
[](undefined) 6158     *          STR
[](undefined) 6159     * By forcing loads from memory (as the asm line causes the compiler to assume
[](undefined) 6160     * that XXH3_kSecretPtr has been changed), the pipelines are used more
[](undefined) 6161     * efficiently:
[](undefined) 6162     *   I   L   S
[](undefined) 6163     *      LDR
[](undefined) 6164     *  ADD LDR
[](undefined) 6165     *  SUB     STR
[](undefined) 6166     *          STR
[](undefined) 6167     *
[](undefined) 6168     * See XXH3_NEON_LANES for details on the pipeline.
[](undefined) 6169     *
[](undefined) 6170     * XXH3_64bits_withSeed, len == 256, Snapdragon 835
[](undefined) 6171     *   without hack: 2654.4 MB/s
[](undefined) 6172     *   with hack:    3202.9 MB/s
[](undefined) 6173     */
[](undefined) 6174    XXH_COMPILER_GUARD(kSecretPtr);
[](undefined) 6175#endif
[](undefined) 6176    {   int const nbRounds = XXH_SECRET_DEFAULT_SIZE / 16;
[](undefined) 6177        int i;
[](undefined) 6178        for (i=0; i < nbRounds; i++) {
[](undefined) 6179            /*
[](undefined) 6180             * The asm hack causes the compiler to assume that kSecretPtr aliases with
[](undefined) 6181             * customSecret, and on aarch64, this prevented LDP from merging two
[](undefined) 6182             * loads together for free. Putting the loads together before the stores
[](undefined) 6183             * properly generates LDP.
[](undefined) 6184             */
[](undefined) 6185            xxh_u64 lo = XXH_readLE64(kSecretPtr + 16*i)     + seed64;
[](undefined) 6186            xxh_u64 hi = XXH_readLE64(kSecretPtr + 16*i + 8) - seed64;
[](undefined) 6187            XXH_writeLE64((xxh_u8*)customSecret + 16*i,     lo);
[](undefined) 6188            XXH_writeLE64((xxh_u8*)customSecret + 16*i + 8, hi);
[](undefined) 6189    }   }
[](undefined) 6190}

[](undefined) 6195typedef void (*XXH3_f_initCustomSecret)(void* XXH_RESTRICT, xxh_u64);

[](undefined) 6271XXH_FORCE_INLINE void
[](undefined) 6272XXH3_hashLong_internal_loop(xxh_u64* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 6273                      const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT input, size_t len,
[](undefined) 6274                      const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret, size_t secretSize,
[](undefined) 6275                            XXH3_f_accumulate f_acc,
[](undefined) 6276                            XXH3_f_scrambleAcc f_scramble)
[](undefined) 6277{
[](undefined) 6278    size_t const nbStripesPerBlock = (secretSize - XXH_STRIPE_LEN) / XXH_SECRET_CONSUME_RATE;
[](undefined) 6279    size_t const block_len = XXH_STRIPE_LEN * nbStripesPerBlock;
[](undefined) 6280    size_t const nb_blocks = (len - 1) / block_len;
[](undefined) 6281
[](undefined) 6282    size_t n;
[](undefined) 6283
[](undefined) 6284    XXH_ASSERT(secretSize >= [XXH3_SECRET_SIZE_MIN](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/14-group___x_x_h3__family/#gaeb79a9111c58af32599f8e5cd71d67d2));
[](undefined) 6285
[](undefined) 6286    for (n = 0; n < nb_blocks; n++) {
[](undefined) 6287        f_acc(acc, input + n*block_len, secret, nbStripesPerBlock);
[](undefined) 6288        f_scramble(acc, secret + secretSize - XXH_STRIPE_LEN);
[](undefined) 6289    }
[](undefined) 6290
[](undefined) 6291    /* last partial block */
[](undefined) 6292    XXH_ASSERT(len > XXH_STRIPE_LEN);
[](undefined) 6293    {   size_t const nbStripes = ((len - 1) - (block_len * nb_blocks)) / XXH_STRIPE_LEN;
[](undefined) 6294        XXH_ASSERT(nbStripes <= (secretSize / XXH_SECRET_CONSUME_RATE));
[](undefined) 6295        f_acc(acc, input + nb_blocks*block_len, secret, nbStripes);
[](undefined) 6296
[](undefined) 6297        /* last stripe */
[](undefined) 6298        {   const xxh_u8* const p = input + len - XXH_STRIPE_LEN;
[](undefined) 6299#define XXH_SECRET_LASTACC_START 7  /* not aligned on 8, last secret is different from acc & scrambler */
[](undefined) 6300            XXH3_accumulate_512(acc, p, secret + secretSize - XXH_STRIPE_LEN - XXH_SECRET_LASTACC_START);
[](undefined) 6301    }   }
[](undefined) 6302}
[](undefined) 6303
[](undefined) 6304XXH_FORCE_INLINE xxh_u64
[](undefined) 6305XXH3_mix2Accs(const xxh_u64* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret)
[](undefined) 6306{
[](undefined) 6307    return XXH3_mul128_fold64(
[](undefined) 6308               acc[0] ^ XXH_readLE64(secret),
[](undefined) 6309               acc[1] ^ XXH_readLE64(secret+8) );
[](undefined) 6310}

[](undefined) 6312static XXH_PUREF [XXH64_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/20-group__public/#ga5406b285b18dfcefa93efed489e3b603)
[](undefined) 6313XXH3_mergeAccs(const xxh_u64* XXH_RESTRICT acc, const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret, xxh_u64 start)
[](undefined) 6314{
[](undefined) 6315    xxh_u64 result64 = start;
[](undefined) 6316    size_t i = 0;
[](undefined) 6317
[](undefined) 6318    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
[](undefined) 6319        result64 += XXH3_mix2Accs(acc+2*i, secret + 16*i);
[](undefined) 6320#if defined(__clang__)                                /* Clang */ \
[](undefined) 6321    && (defined(__arm__) || defined(__thumb__))       /* ARMv7 */ \
[](undefined) 6322    && (defined(__ARM_NEON) || defined(__ARM_NEON__)) /* NEON */  \
[](undefined) 6323    && !defined(XXH_ENABLE_AUTOVECTORIZE)             /* Define to disable */
[](undefined) 6324        /*
[](undefined) 6325         * UGLY HACK:
[](undefined) 6326         * Prevent autovectorization on Clang ARMv7-a. Exact same problem as
[](undefined) 6327         * the one in XXH3_len_129to240_64b. Speeds up shorter keys > 240b.
[](undefined) 6328         * XXH3_64bits, len == 256, Snapdragon 835:
[](undefined) 6329         *   without hack: 2063.7 MB/s
[](undefined) 6330         *   with hack:    2560.7 MB/s
[](undefined) 6331         */
[](undefined) 6332        XXH_COMPILER_GUARD(result64);
[](undefined) 6333#endif
[](undefined) 6334    }
[](undefined) 6335
[](undefined) 6336    return XXH3_avalanche(result64);
[](undefined) 6337}

[](undefined) 6497/* ===   XXH3 streaming   === */
[](undefined) 6498#ifndef XXH_NO_STREAM
[](undefined) 6499/*
[](undefined) 6500 * Malloc's a pointer that is always aligned to @align.
[](undefined) 6501 *
[](undefined) 6502 * This must be freed with `XXH_alignedFree()`.
[](undefined) 6503 *
[](undefined) 6504 * malloc typically guarantees 16 byte alignment on 64-bit systems and 8 byte
[](undefined) 6505 * alignment on 32-bit. This isn't enough for the 32 byte aligned loads in AVX2
[](undefined) 6506 * or on 32-bit, the 16 byte aligned loads in SSE2 and NEON.
[](undefined) 6507 *
[](undefined) 6508 * This underalignment previously caused a rather obvious crash which went
[](undefined) 6509 * completely unnoticed due to XXH3_createState() not actually being tested.
[](undefined) 6510 * Credit to RedSpah for noticing this bug.
[](undefined) 6511 *
[](undefined) 6512 * The alignment is done manually: Functions like posix_memalign or _mm_malloc
[](undefined) 6513 * are avoided: To maintain portability, we would have to write a fallback
[](undefined) 6514 * like this anyways, and besides, testing for the existence of library
[](undefined) 6515 * functions without relying on external build tools is impossible.
[](undefined) 6516 *
[](undefined) 6517 * The method is simple: Overallocate, manually align, and store the offset
[](undefined) 6518 * to the original behind the returned pointer.
[](undefined) 6519 *
[](undefined) 6520 * Align must be a power of 2 and 8 <= align <= 128.
[](undefined) 6521 */
[](undefined) 6522static XXH_MALLOCF void* XXH_alignedMalloc(size_t s, size_t align)
[](undefined) 6523{
[](undefined) 6524    XXH_ASSERT(align <= 128 && align >= 8); /* range check */
[](undefined) 6525    XXH_ASSERT((align & (align-1)) == 0);   /* power of 2 */
[](undefined) 6526    XXH_ASSERT(s != 0 && s < (s + align));  /* empty/overflow */
[](undefined) 6527    {   /* Overallocate to make room for manual realignment and an offset byte */
[](undefined) 6528        xxh_u8* base = (xxh_u8*)XXH_malloc(s + align);
[](undefined) 6529        if (base != NULL) {
[](undefined) 6530            /*
[](undefined) 6531             * Get the offset needed to align this pointer.
[](undefined) 6532             *
[](undefined) 6533             * Even if the returned pointer is aligned, there will always be
[](undefined) 6534             * at least one byte to store the offset to the original pointer.
[](undefined) 6535             */
[](undefined) 6536            size_t offset = align - ((size_t)base & (align - 1)); /* base % align */
[](undefined) 6537            /* Add the offset for the now-aligned pointer */
[](undefined) 6538            xxh_u8* ptr = base + offset;
[](undefined) 6539
[](undefined) 6540            XXH_ASSERT((size_t)ptr % align == 0);
[](undefined) 6541
[](undefined) 6542            /* Store the offset immediately before the returned pointer. */
[](undefined) 6543            ptr[-1] = (xxh_u8)offset;
[](undefined) 6544            return ptr;
[](undefined) 6545        }
[](undefined) 6546        return NULL;
[](undefined) 6547    }
[](undefined) 6548}
[](undefined) 6549/*
[](undefined) 6550 * Frees an aligned pointer allocated by XXH_alignedMalloc(). Don't pass
[](undefined) 6551 * normal malloc'd pointers, XXH_alignedMalloc has a specific data layout.
[](undefined) 6552 */
[](undefined) 6553static void XXH_alignedFree(void* p)
[](undefined) 6554{
[](undefined) 6555    if (p != NULL) {
[](undefined) 6556        xxh_u8* ptr = (xxh_u8*)p;
[](undefined) 6557        /* Get the offset byte we added in XXH_malloc. */
[](undefined) 6558        xxh_u8 offset = ptr[-1];
[](undefined) 6559        /* Free the original malloc'd pointer */
[](undefined) 6560        xxh_u8* base = ptr - offset;
[](undefined) 6561        XXH_free(base);
[](undefined) 6562    }
[](undefined) 6563}

[](undefined) 6678
[](undefined) 6696XXH_FORCE_INLINE const xxh_u8 *
[](undefined) 6697XXH3_consumeStripes(xxh_u64* XXH_RESTRICT acc,
[](undefined) 6698                    size_t* XXH_RESTRICT nbStripesSoFarPtr, size_t nbStripesPerBlock,
[](undefined) 6699                    const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT input, size_t nbStripes,
[](undefined) 6700                    const xxh_u8* XXH_RESTRICT secret, size_t secretLimit,
[](undefined) 6701                    XXH3_f_accumulate f_acc,
[](undefined) 6702                    XXH3_f_scrambleAcc f_scramble)
[](undefined) 6703{
[](undefined) 6704    const xxh_u8* initialSecret = secret + *nbStripesSoFarPtr * XXH_SECRET_CONSUME_RATE;
[](undefined) 6705    /* Process full blocks */
[](undefined) 6706    if (nbStripes >= (nbStripesPerBlock - *nbStripesSoFarPtr)) {
[](undefined) 6707        /* Process the initial partial block... */
[](undefined) 6708        size_t nbStripesThisIter = nbStripesPerBlock - *nbStripesSoFarPtr;
[](undefined) 6709
[](undefined) 6710        do {
[](undefined) 6711            /* Accumulate and scramble */
[](undefined) 6712            f_acc(acc, input, initialSecret, nbStripesThisIter);
[](undefined) 6713            f_scramble(acc, secret + secretLimit);
[](undefined) 6714            input += nbStripesThisIter * XXH_STRIPE_LEN;
[](undefined) 6715            nbStripes -= nbStripesThisIter;
[](undefined) 6716            /* Then continue the loop with the full block size */
[](undefined) 6717            nbStripesThisIter = nbStripesPerBlock;
[](undefined) 6718            initialSecret = secret;
[](undefined) 6719        } while (nbStripes >= nbStripesPerBlock);
[](undefined) 6720        *nbStripesSoFarPtr = 0;
[](undefined) 6721    }
[](undefined) 6722    /* Process a partial block */
[](undefined) 6723    if (nbStripes > 0) {
[](undefined) 6724        f_acc(acc, input, initialSecret, nbStripes);
[](undefined) 6725        input += nbStripes * XXH_STRIPE_LEN;
[](undefined) 6726        *nbStripesSoFarPtr += nbStripes;
[](undefined) 6727    }
[](undefined) 6728    /* Return end pointer */
[](undefined) 6729    return input;
[](undefined) 6730}
[](undefined) 6731
[](undefined) 6732#ifndef XXH3_STREAM_USE_STACK
[](undefined) 6733# if XXH_SIZE_OPT <= 0 && !defined(__clang__) /* clang doesn't need additional stack space */
[](undefined) 6734#   define XXH3_STREAM_USE_STACK 1
[](undefined) 6735# endif
[](undefined) 6736#endif
[](undefined) 6737/* This function accepts f_acc and f_scramble as function pointers,
[](undefined) 6738 * making it possible to implement multiple variants with different acc & scramble stages.
[](undefined) 6739 * This is notably useful to implement multiple vector variants with different intrinsics.

[](undefined) 6893
[](undefined) 6894/* ==========================================
[](undefined) 6895 * XXH3 128 bits (a.k.a XXH128)
[](undefined) 6896 * ==========================================
[](undefined) 6897 * XXH3's 128-bit variant has better mixing and strength than the 64-bit variant,
[](undefined) 6898 * even without counting the significantly larger output size.
[](undefined) 6899 *
[](undefined) 6900 * For example, extra steps are taken to mitigate the seed-dependent collisions
[](undefined) 6901 * in 17-240 byte inputs (See XXH3_mix16B and XXH128_mix32B). Note that these
[](undefined) 6902 * steps only mitigate, they do not eliminate the issue: see the DISCLAIMER
[](undefined) 6903 * above XXH3_mix16B() for the cases that get through.
[](undefined) 6904 *
[](undefined) 6905 * This strength naturally comes at the cost of some speed, especially on short
[](undefined) 6906 * lengths. Note that longer hashes are about as fast as the 64-bit version
[](undefined) 6907 * due to it using only a slight modification of the 64-bit loop.
[](undefined) 6908 *
[](undefined) 6909 * XXH128 is also more oriented towards 64-bit machines. It is still extremely
[](undefined) 6910 * fast for a _128-bit_ hash on 32-bit (it usually clears XXH64).
[](undefined) 6911 */
[](undefined) 6912
[](undefined) 6913XXH_FORCE_INLINE XXH_PUREF [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/)
[](undefined) 6914XXH3_len_1to3_128b(const xxh_u8* input, size_t len, const xxh_u8* secret, [XXH64_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/20-group__public/#ga5406b285b18dfcefa93efed489e3b603) seed)
[](undefined) 6915{
[](undefined) 6916    /* A doubled version of 1to3_64b with different constants. */
[](undefined) 6917    XXH_ASSERT(input != NULL);
[](undefined) 6918    XXH_ASSERT(1 <= len && len <= 3);
[](undefined) 6919    XXH_ASSERT(secret != NULL);
[](undefined) 6920    /*
[](undefined) 6921     * len = 1: combinedl = { input[0], 0x01, input[0], input[0] }
[](undefined) 6922     * len = 2: combinedl = { input[1], 0x02, input[0], input[1] }
[](undefined) 6923     * len = 3: combinedl = { input[2], 0x03, input[0], input[1] }
[](undefined) 6924     */
[](undefined) 6925    {   xxh_u8 const c1 = input[0];
[](undefined) 6926        xxh_u8 const c2 = input[len >> 1];
[](undefined) 6927        xxh_u8 const c3 = input[len - 1];
[](undefined) 6928        xxh_u32 const combinedl = ((xxh_u32)c1 <<16) | ((xxh_u32)c2 << 24)
[](undefined) 6929                                | ((xxh_u32)c3 << 0) | ((xxh_u32)len << 8);
[](undefined) 6930        xxh_u32 const combinedh = XXH_rotl32(XXH_swap32(combinedl), 13);
[](undefined) 6931        xxh_u64 const bitflipl = (XXH_readLE32(secret) ^ XXH_readLE32(secret+4)) + seed;
[](undefined) 6932        xxh_u64 const bitfliph = (XXH_readLE32(secret+8) ^ XXH_readLE32(secret+12)) - seed;
[](undefined) 6933        xxh_u64 const keyed_lo = (xxh_u64)combinedl ^ bitflipl;
[](undefined) 6934        xxh_u64 const keyed_hi = (xxh_u64)combinedh ^ bitfliph;
[](undefined) 6935        [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/) h128;
[](undefined) 6936        h128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef)  = XXH64_avalanche(keyed_lo);
[](undefined) 6937        h128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) = XXH64_avalanche(keyed_hi);
[](undefined) 6938        return h128;
[](undefined) 6939    }
[](undefined) 6940}
[](undefined) 6941
[](undefined) 6942XXH_FORCE_INLINE XXH_PUREF [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/)
[](undefined) 6943XXH3_len_4to8_128b(const xxh_u8* input, size_t len, const xxh_u8* secret, [XXH64_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/20-group__public/#ga5406b285b18dfcefa93efed489e3b603) seed)
[](undefined) 6944{
[](undefined) 6945    XXH_ASSERT(input != NULL);
[](undefined) 6946    XXH_ASSERT(secret != NULL);
[](undefined) 6947    XXH_ASSERT(4 <= len && len <= 8);
[](undefined) 6948    seed ^= (xxh_u64)XXH_swap32((xxh_u32)seed) << 32;
[](undefined) 6949    {   xxh_u32 const input_lo = XXH_readLE32(input);
[](undefined) 6950        xxh_u32 const input_hi = XXH_readLE32(input + len - 4);
[](undefined) 6951        xxh_u64 const input_64 = input_lo + ((xxh_u64)input_hi << 32);
[](undefined) 6952        xxh_u64 const bitflip = (XXH_readLE64(secret+16) ^ XXH_readLE64(secret+24)) + seed;
[](undefined) 6953        xxh_u64 const keyed = input_64 ^ bitflip;
[](undefined) 6954
[](undefined) 6955        /* Shift len to the left to ensure it is even, this avoids even multiplies. */
[](undefined) 6956        [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/) m128 = XXH_mult64to128(keyed, [XXH_PRIME64_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/17-group___x_x_h64__impl/#ga20651a9ad53cae6f0a4feb1393d5a716) + (len << 2));
[](undefined) 6957
[](undefined) 6958        m128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) += (m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef) << 1);
[](undefined) 6959        m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef)  ^= (m128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) >> 3);
[](undefined) 6960
[](undefined) 6961        m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef)   = XXH_xorshift64(m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef), 35);
[](undefined) 6962        m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef)  *= PRIME_MX2;

[](undefined) 6969XXH_FORCE_INLINE XXH_PUREF [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/)
[](undefined) 6970XXH3_len_9to16_128b(const xxh_u8* input, size_t len, const xxh_u8* secret, [XXH64_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/20-group__public/#ga5406b285b18dfcefa93efed489e3b603) seed)
[](undefined) 6971{
[](undefined) 6972    XXH_ASSERT(input != NULL);
[](undefined) 6973    XXH_ASSERT(secret != NULL);
[](undefined) 6974    XXH_ASSERT(9 <= len && len <= 16);
[](undefined) 6975    {   xxh_u64 const bitflipl = (XXH_readLE64(secret+32) ^ XXH_readLE64(secret+40)) - seed;
[](undefined) 6976        xxh_u64 const bitfliph = (XXH_readLE64(secret+48) ^ XXH_readLE64(secret+56)) + seed;
[](undefined) 6977        xxh_u64 const input_lo = XXH_readLE64(input);
[](undefined) 6978        xxh_u64       input_hi = XXH_readLE64(input + len - 8);
[](undefined) 6979        [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/) m128 = XXH_mult64to128(input_lo ^ input_hi ^ bitflipl, [XXH_PRIME64_1](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/17-group___x_x_h64__impl/#ga20651a9ad53cae6f0a4feb1393d5a716));
[](undefined) 6980        /*
[](undefined) 6981         * Put len in the middle of m128 to ensure that the length gets mixed to
[](undefined) 6982         * both the low and high bits in the 128x64 multiply below.
[](undefined) 6983         */
[](undefined) 6984        m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef) += (xxh_u64)(len - 1) << 54;
[](undefined) 6985        input_hi   ^= bitfliph;
[](undefined) 6986        /*
[](undefined) 6987         * Add the high 32 bits of input_hi to the high 32 bits of m128, then
[](undefined) 6988         * add the long product of the low 32 bits of input_hi and XXH_PRIME32_2 to
[](undefined) 6989         * the high 64 bits of m128.
[](undefined) 6990         *
[](undefined) 6991         * The best approach to this operation is different on 32-bit and 64-bit.
[](undefined) 6992         */
[](undefined) 6993        if (sizeof(void *) < sizeof(xxh_u64)) { /* 32-bit */
[](undefined) 6994            /*
[](undefined) 6995             * 32-bit optimized version, which is more readable.
[](undefined) 6996             *
[](undefined) 6997             * On 32-bit, it removes an ADC and delays a dependency between the two
[](undefined) 6998             * halves of m128.high64, but it generates an extra mask on 64-bit.
[](undefined) 6999             */
[](undefined) 7000            m128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) += (input_hi & 0xFFFFFFFF00000000ULL) + XXH_mult32to64((xxh_u32)input_hi, [XXH_PRIME32_2](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga23d49e684c0551afaed8e4ff40940c79));
[](undefined) 7001        } else {
[](undefined) 7002            /*
[](undefined) 7003             * 64-bit optimized (albeit more confusing) version.
[](undefined) 7004             *
[](undefined) 7005             * Uses some properties of addition and multiplication to remove the mask:
[](undefined) 7006             *
[](undefined) 7007             * Let:
[](undefined) 7008             *    a = input_hi.lo = (input_hi & 0x00000000FFFFFFFF)
[](undefined) 7009             *    b = input_hi.hi = (input_hi & 0xFFFFFFFF00000000)
[](undefined) 7010             *    c = XXH_PRIME32_2
[](undefined) 7011             *
[](undefined) 7012             *    a + (b * c)
[](undefined) 7013             * Inverse Property: x + y - x == y
[](undefined) 7014             *    a + (b * (1 + c - 1))
[](undefined) 7015             * Distributive Property: x * (y + z) == (x * y) + (x * z)
[](undefined) 7016             *    a + (b * 1) + (b * (c - 1))
[](undefined) 7017             * Identity Property: x * 1 == x
[](undefined) 7018             *    a + b + (b * (c - 1))
[](undefined) 7019             *
[](undefined) 7020             * Substitute a, b, and c:
[](undefined) 7021             *    input_hi.hi + input_hi.lo + ((xxh_u64)input_hi.lo * (XXH_PRIME32_2 - 1))
[](undefined) 7022             *
[](undefined) 7023             * Since input_hi.hi + input_hi.lo == input_hi, we get this:
[](undefined) 7024             *    input_hi + ((xxh_u64)input_hi.lo * (XXH_PRIME32_2 - 1))
[](undefined) 7025             */
[](undefined) 7026            m128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) += input_hi + XXH_mult32to64((xxh_u32)input_hi, [XXH_PRIME32_2](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/13-group___x_x_h32__impl/#ga23d49e684c0551afaed8e4ff40940c79) - 1);
[](undefined) 7027        }
[](undefined) 7028        /* m128 ^= XXH_swap64(m128 >> 64); */
[](undefined) 7029        m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef)  ^= XXH_swap64(m128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8));
[](undefined) 7030
[](undefined) 7031        {   /* 128x64 multiply: h128 = m128 * XXH_PRIME64_2; */
[](undefined) 7032            [XXH128_hash_t](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/) h128 = XXH_mult64to128(m128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef), [XXH_PRIME64_2](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/17-group___x_x_h64__impl/#ga33a01ca165b68cef76da009a54e3ba54));
[](undefined) 7033            h128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) += m128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8) * [XXH_PRIME64_2](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/17-group___x_x_h64__impl/#ga33a01ca165b68cef76da009a54e3ba54);
[](undefined) 7034
[](undefined) 7035            h128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef)   = XXH3_avalanche(h128.[low64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#a706323c9b3e76414ec77f8f93e8644ef));
[](undefined) 7036            h128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8)  = XXH3_avalanche(h128.[high64](/docs/xxhash/v0-8-4/en/02-api/24-struct_x_x_h128__hash__t/#ad4e0c851eb11bd3f8042ceebc15fe0a8));
